<P STYLE="font: 9pt Gotham Rounded Book; margin: 6pt 0; text-align: justify; color: #0F2B89"><B>Central Puerto 2<SUP>nd</SUP> Quarter
2025 Financial and Operational Results</B></P>

<P STYLE="font: 9pt/115% Gotham Rounded Book; margin: 6pt 0; text-align: justify">Buenos Aires, <B>August 11<SUP>th</SUP></B>, Central
Puerto S.A (&ldquo;Central Puerto&rdquo; or the &ldquo;Company&rdquo;) (NYSE and BYMA: CEPU), the largest private sector power generation
companies in Argentina, reports its consolidated financial results for the <B>Second quarter 2025 (</B>&ldquo;2Q25&rdquo;), ended on June
30<SUP>th</SUP>, 2025.</P>

<P STYLE="font: 9pt Gotham Rounded Book; margin: 0; text-align: justify">A conference call to discuss the results of the second quarter
of 2025 will be held today <B>at 12:00 PM ET Eastern Time</B>. <B>Webcast Access:</B> Click here<SUP>1</SUP>.</P>

<P STYLE="font: 9pt/115% Gotham Rounded Book; margin: 6pt 0; text-align: justify"></P>

<P STYLE="font: 9pt Gotham Rounded Book; margin: 6pt 0; text-align: justify; color: #0F2B89"><B>Highlights of the quarter</B></P>

<TABLE CELLPADDING="0" CELLSPACING="0" WIDTH="100%" STYLE="font: 12pt/115% Trebuchet MS, Times, Serif; margin-top: 6pt; margin-bottom: 6pt"><TR STYLE="vertical-align: top">
<TD STYLE="width: 0.25in"></TD><TD STYLE="width: 0.25in"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Symbol; font-size: 9pt">&#183;</FONT></TD><TD STYLE="text-align: justify"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Gotham Rounded Book; font-size: 9pt">2Q25 Adjusted EBITDA was US$ 61.4 MM, a
-32% compared to the US$ 89.9 MM Adjusted EBITDA in 1Q25, and +35% compared to 2Q24 Adjusted EBITDA of US$ 45.6 MM. </FONT></TD></TR></TABLE>

<TABLE CELLPADDING="0" CELLSPACING="0" WIDTH="100%" STYLE="font: 12pt/115% Trebuchet MS, Times, Serif; margin-top: 6pt; margin-bottom: 6pt"><TR STYLE="vertical-align: top">
<TD STYLE="width: 0.25in"></TD><TD STYLE="width: 0.25in"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Symbol; font-size: 9pt; color: windowtext">&#183;</FONT></TD><TD STYLE="text-align: justify"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Gotham Rounded Book; font-size: 9pt">Total generation volumes in 2Q25 reached
4,372 GWh, representing a 24% decrease compared to 1Q25 (5,731 GWh) and a 12% decline versus 2Q24 (4,985 GWh). <FONT STYLE="color: windowtext">Lower
volumes are explained mainly by the maintenance work at the Mitsubishi combined cycle of Central Costanera which generated 409 GWh in
2Q25, 75% below from 1Q25 (1,612 GWh) and 57% lower than 2Q24 (961 GWh) and the maintenance of the ST06 (TV06) of Central Puerto complex
which generated 5.3 GWh in 2Q25 vs 191.8 GWh in 1Q25 and 90.3 GWh in 2Q24.</FONT></FONT></TD></TR></TABLE>

<TABLE CELLPADDING="0" CELLSPACING="0" WIDTH="100%" STYLE="font: 12pt/115% Trebuchet MS, Times, Serif; margin-top: 6pt; margin-bottom: 6pt"><TR STYLE="vertical-align: top">
<TD STYLE="width: 0.25in"></TD><TD STYLE="width: 0.25in"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Symbol; font-size: 9pt">&#183;</FONT></TD><TD STYLE="text-align: justify"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Gotham Rounded Book; font-size: 9pt">Revenues in 2Q25 totalized US$ 179.6 MM,
which represented an 8% decrease compared to 1Q25 (US$ 196.2 MM) and an increase of 7% compared to 2Q24 (US$ 168.3 MM). Revenues from
energy sales represented 89.6% out of total revenues. Energy sales at 2Q25 were US$ 160.9 MM, a 10% decrease compared to 1Q25 (US$ 179.3
MM), and a 9% increase compared to 2Q24 (US$ 147.2 MM). The q/q variation was <FONT STYLE="color: windowtext">primarily due to seasonal
capacity charges (US$ -19.1 MM q/q) and lower volumes sold mainly from the maintenance work at the Mitsubishi combined cycle of Central
Costanera and ST06 (TV06) from Central Puerto complex (US$ -12.4 MM q/q), partly compensated by additional revenues from self-procured
fuels for Luj&aacute;n de Cuyo plan, and other fuels as established by Resol. SE N&deg; 21/25 (+US$ 13.8 MM q/q). </FONT></FONT></TD></TR></TABLE>

<TABLE CELLPADDING="0" CELLSPACING="0" WIDTH="100%" STYLE="font: 12pt/115% Trebuchet MS, Times, Serif; margin-top: 6pt; margin-bottom: 6pt"><TR STYLE="vertical-align: top">
<TD STYLE="width: 0.25in"></TD><TD STYLE="width: 0.25in"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Symbol; font-size: 9pt">&#183;</FONT></TD><TD STYLE="text-align: justify"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Gotham Rounded Book; font-size: 9pt">Spot prices: In 2Q25, the Energy Secretariat
approved AR$-denominated spot price adjustments totaling 5.1% on a compound basis as of June. This compares to a 6.0% increase in the
wholesale inflation index and 11.6% variation in the exchange rate over the same period. Since January 2025, the Energy Secretariat has
implemented AR$-denominated spot price adjustments totaling 15.4% on a compound basis as of June. This compares to a 15.1% increase in
the wholesale inflation index and a 15.7% variation in the exchange rate over the same period. </FONT></TD></TR></TABLE>

<TABLE CELLPADDING="0" CELLSPACING="0" WIDTH="100%" STYLE="font: 12pt/115% Trebuchet MS, Times, Serif; margin-top: 6pt; margin-bottom: 6pt"><TR STYLE="vertical-align: top">
<TD STYLE="width: 0.25in"></TD><TD STYLE="width: 0.25in"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Symbol; font-size: 9pt">&#183;</FONT></TD><TD STYLE="text-align: justify"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Gotham Rounded Book; font-size: 9pt">Capital expenditures in 1Q25 and 2Q25 totaled
US$ 102.4 MM, mainly allocated to the closing of Brigadier Lopez combined cycle and the San Carlos solar project, the capex for the Mitsubishi
CC and additional capital subscription of AbraSilver.</FONT></TD></TR></TABLE>

<TABLE CELLPADDING="0" CELLSPACING="0" WIDTH="100%" STYLE="font: 12pt/115% Trebuchet MS, Times, Serif; margin-top: 6pt; margin-bottom: 6pt"><TR STYLE="vertical-align: top">
<TD STYLE="width: 0.25in"></TD><TD STYLE="width: 0.25in"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Symbol; font-size: 9pt">&#183;</FONT></TD><TD STYLE="text-align: justify"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Gotham Rounded Book; font-size: 9pt">As of June 30, 2025, cash and cash equivalents
and current financial assets balance at the end of the period was US$ 235,2 MM (cash and cash equivalents totaling US$3.7 MM, while other
current financial assets amounted to US$ 231.5 MM). The total outstanding gross debt balance as of June 30th, 2025, was US$ 409.4 MM.
As of June 30<SUP>th</SUP>, 2025, net financial debt resulted in US$ 174.2 MM and LTM Adjusted EBITDA was US$ 309.9 MM. Net leverage ratio
was 0.56x Adj. EBITDA.</FONT></TD></TR></TABLE>

<TABLE CELLPADDING="0" CELLSPACING="0" WIDTH="100%" STYLE="font: 11pt/115% Calibri, Helvetica, Sans-Serif; margin-top: 6pt; margin-bottom: 6pt; color: #0F2B89"><TR STYLE="vertical-align: top">
<TD STYLE="width: 18pt"></TD><TD STYLE="width: 19.5pt"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Gotham Rounded Book; font-size: 9pt"><B>A.</B></FONT></TD><TD STYLE="text-align: justify"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Gotham Rounded Book; font-size: 9pt"><B>Regulatory updates and analysis</B></FONT></TD></TR></TABLE>

<P STYLE="font: 9pt/115% Gotham Rounded Book; margin: 6pt 0 6pt 37.5pt; text-align: justify; color: #0F2B89"><B></B></P>

<TABLE CELLPADDING="0" CELLSPACING="0" WIDTH="100%" STYLE="font: 9pt/115% Calibri, Helvetica, Sans-Serif; margin-top: 6pt; margin-bottom: 6pt"><TR STYLE="vertical-align: top">
<TD STYLE="width: 0"></TD><TD STYLE="width: 7.1pt"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Wingdings">&#167;</FONT></TD><TD STYLE="text-align: justify"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Gotham Rounded Book"><U>Energy regulatory framework reform announcements</U></FONT></TD></TR></TABLE>

<P STYLE="font: 9pt Gotham Rounded Book; margin: 0; text-align: justify">On July 4<SUP>th</SUP>, 2025, in accordance with Sections 161
and 162 of Law No. 27,742 on Bases and Starting Points for the Freedom of Argentines (the &ldquo;Bases Law&rdquo;), the National Executive
Branch issued Decrees No. 450/2025, No. 451/2025, and No. 452/2025.</P>

<P STYLE="font: 9pt Gotham Rounded Book; margin: 0; text-align: justify">&nbsp;</P>

<P STYLE="font: 7pt Gotham Rounded Book; margin: 0"></P>

<P STYLE="font: 12pt Calibri, Helvetica, Sans-Serif; margin: 0; text-align: justify">&nbsp;</P>

<P STYLE="font: 9pt Gotham Rounded Book; margin: 0; text-align: justify">Decree No. 450/2025 approved amendments to Law No. 15,336 and
Law No. 24,065, which regulate the electricity sector. Decree No. 451/2025 approved adjustments to Law No. 24,076, concerning natural
gas Law No. 24,076. Decree No. 452/2025 established the National Gas and Electricity Regulatory Entity, a new body that consolidates the
regulatory functions previously exercised by ENARGAS and ENRE.</P>

<P STYLE="font: 4pt Gotham Rounded Book; margin: 0; text-align: justify">&nbsp;</P>

<TABLE CELLPADDING="0" CELLSPACING="0" WIDTH="100%" STYLE="font: 9pt/115% Calibri, Helvetica, Sans-Serif; margin-top: 6pt; margin-bottom: 6pt"><TR STYLE="vertical-align: top">
<TD STYLE="width: 0"></TD><TD STYLE="width: 7.1pt"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Wingdings">&#167;</FONT></TD><TD STYLE="text-align: justify"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Gotham Rounded Book"><U>Piedra del &Aacute;guila concession</U></FONT></TD></TR></TABLE>

<P STYLE="font: 9pt Gotham Rounded Book; margin: 6pt 0; text-align: justify">On August 7, 2025, Argentina's Executive Branch (PEN) issued
Decree No. 476/2025. The decree establishes a new set of terms and a required payment for an Adhesion Agreement. It also grants an additional
90-day period for the current concession, which can be extended until the end of the year.</P>

<TABLE CELLPADDING="0" CELLSPACING="0" WIDTH="100%" STYLE="font: 9pt/115% Calibri, Helvetica, Sans-Serif; margin-top: 6pt; margin-bottom: 6pt"><TR STYLE="vertical-align: top">
<TD STYLE="width: 0"></TD><TD STYLE="width: 7.1pt"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Wingdings">&#167;</FONT></TD><TD STYLE="text-align: justify"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Gotham Rounded Book"><U>AR peso denominated electricity spot prices adjustments
in 2025</U>: </FONT></TD></TR></TABLE>

<P STYLE="font: 9pt Gotham Rounded Book; margin: 6pt 0; text-align: justify">Since January 2025, the Energy Secretariat has implemented
AR$-denominated spot price adjustments totaling 15.4% on a compound basis through June (1<SUP>st</SUP> semester, 2025). In the same semester,
the compound increase in the wholesale inflation index was 15.1% and the variation in the AR$/US$ exchange rate was 15.7%.</P>

<P STYLE="font: 9pt Gotham Rounded Book; margin: 6pt 0; text-align: justify">Analysis of AR$-denominated spot prices compared to inflation
and FX variation:</P>

<TABLE CELLSPACING="0" CELLPADDING="0" STYLE="font: 12pt Calibri, Helvetica, Sans-Serif; width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse">
  <TR STYLE="vertical-align: top">
    <TD STYLE="border-top: Black 1pt solid; width: 52%; border-bottom: Black 1pt solid; padding-top: 6pt; padding-right: 5.4pt; padding-left: 5.4pt; text-align: justify"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Gotham Rounded Book; font-size: 8pt; color: #002060">AR$-denominated spot prices adjustments</FONT></TD>
    <TD STYLE="border-top: Black 1pt solid; width: 17%; border-bottom: Black 1pt solid; padding-top: 6pt; padding-right: 5.4pt; padding-left: 5.4pt; text-align: center"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Gotham Rounded Book; font-size: 8pt; color: #002060">2Q 2025</FONT></TD>
    <TD STYLE="border-top: Black 1pt solid; width: 14%; border-bottom: Black 1pt solid; padding-top: 6pt; padding-right: 5.4pt; padding-left: 5.4pt; text-align: center"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Gotham Rounded Book; font-size: 8pt; color: #002060">1Q 2025</FONT></TD>
    <TD STYLE="border-top: Black 1pt solid; width: 17%; border-bottom: Black 1pt solid; padding-top: 6pt; padding-right: 5.4pt; padding-left: 5.4pt; text-align: center"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Gotham Rounded Book; font-size: 8pt; color: #002060">1H 2025</FONT></TD></TR>
  <TR STYLE="vertical-align: top">
    <TD STYLE="padding-top: 6pt; padding-right: 5.4pt; padding-left: 5.4pt; text-align: justify"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Gotham Rounded Book; font-size: 8pt">&nbsp;Spot prices adjustments <SUP>(1)</SUP></FONT></TD>
    <TD STYLE="padding-top: 6pt; padding-right: 5.4pt; padding-left: 5.4pt; text-align: center"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Gotham Rounded Book; font-size: 8pt">5.1%</FONT></TD>
    <TD STYLE="padding-top: 6pt; padding-right: 5.4pt; padding-left: 5.4pt; text-align: center"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Gotham Rounded Book; font-size: 8pt">9.8%</FONT></TD>
    <TD STYLE="padding-top: 6pt; padding-right: 5.4pt; padding-left: 5.4pt; text-align: center"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Gotham Rounded Book; font-size: 8pt">15.4%</FONT></TD></TR>
  <TR STYLE="vertical-align: top">

    <TD STYLE="padding-top: 6pt; padding-right: 5.4pt; padding-left: 5.4pt; text-align: center"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Gotham Rounded Book; font-size: 8pt">15.1%</FONT></TD></TR>
  <TR STYLE="vertical-align: top">

    <TD STYLE="border-bottom: Black 1pt solid; padding-top: 6pt; padding-right: 5.4pt; padding-left: 5.4pt; text-align: center"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Gotham Rounded Book; font-size: 8pt">11.6%</FONT></TD>

    <TD STYLE="border-bottom: Black 1pt solid; padding-top: 6pt; padding-right: 5.4pt; padding-left: 5.4pt; text-align: center"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Gotham Rounded Book; font-size: 8pt">15.7%</FONT></TD></TR>

<P STYLE="font: 7pt Gotham Rounded Book; margin: 0; text-align: justify; color: #2A2A2A"><FONT STYLE="background-color: white">(1) Certain

<P STYLE="font: 9pt Gotham Rounded Book; margin: 0; text-align: justify">&nbsp;</P>

<P STYLE="font: 9pt Calibri, Helvetica, Sans-Serif; margin: 0 0 10pt; text-align: justify; text-indent: 0in"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Wingdings">&#167;</FONT>
<FONT STYLE="font-family: Gotham Rounded Book">In addition, we would also like to highlight <U>Resolution SE No. 21/25</U>. Although the
resolution was issued at the end of January, the self-managed fuel procurement scheme was established to begin in March 2025. As a result,
its full impact was reflected in the current quarter, which we will discuss in more detail in the following sections.</FONT></P>

<P STYLE="font: 9pt Gotham Rounded Book; margin: 0; text-align: justify"></P>

<P STYLE="font: 9pt Gotham Rounded Book; margin: 0; text-align: justify">&nbsp;</P>

<TABLE CELLPADDING="0" CELLSPACING="0" WIDTH="100%" STYLE="font: 11pt/115% Calibri, Helvetica, Sans-Serif; margin-top: 6pt; margin-bottom: 6pt; color: #0F2B89"><TR STYLE="vertical-align: top">
<TD STYLE="width: 18pt"></TD><TD STYLE="width: 19.5pt"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Gotham Rounded Book; font-size: 9pt"><B>B.</B></FONT></TD><TD STYLE="text-align: justify"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Gotham Rounded Book; font-size: 9pt"><B>Electricity market balance and trends</B></FONT></TD></TR></TABLE>

<P STYLE="font: 9pt Gotham Rounded Book; margin-right: 0; margin-left: 0">The total system installed capacity remained stable both quarter-over-quarter
and year-over-year, with a 3% increase in renewable capacity compared to the previous quarter. The 11% year-on-year decrease in hydro
capacity (from Yacireta&rsquo;s bi-national plant generation re-assignment agreement) was offset by a 21% increase in renewables.</P>

<P STYLE="font: 9pt Gotham Rounded Book; margin-right: 0; margin-left: 0">Offer: Thermal generation decreased 24.5% q/q while hydro generation
increased 14.9% q/q.</P>


<!-- Field: Page; Sequence: 4 -->
    <DIV STYLE="margin-bottom: 6pt; border-bottom: Black 1pt solid"><TABLE CELLPADDING="0" CELLSPACING="0" STYLE="width: 100%"><TR><TD STYLE="text-align: center">&nbsp;</TD></TR><TR><TD STYLE="text-align: center; width: 100%"><P STYLE="font: 9pt Gotham Rounded Light; margin: 0"><B>Central Puerto financial and operational results for 2Q 2025</B></P></TD></TR></TABLE></DIV>
    <DIV STYLE="break-before: page; margin-top: 6pt"><TABLE CELLPADDING="0" CELLSPACING="0" STYLE="width: 100%"><TR><TD STYLE="text-align: left; width: 100%"><IMG SRC="cepupr2q256k_002.jpg" ALT="">&nbsp;</TD></TR><TR><TD STYLE="text-align: left">&nbsp;</TD></TR></TABLE></DIV>
    <!-- Field: /Page -->

<P STYLE="font: 9pt Gotham Rounded Book; margin: 6pt 0; text-align: justify">Demand: Electricity demand declined by 12% in 2Q25 compared
to 1Q25, primarily due to milder temperatures. This resulted in lower energy consumption from the residential (-15.7% q/q) and commercial
(-13.7% q/q) segments&mdash;a seasonal trend typically expected in the autumn months compared to summer.</P>

<P STYLE="font: 12pt Calibri, Helvetica, Sans-Serif; margin: 6pt 0; text-align: justify">&nbsp;</P>

<TABLE CELLPADDING="0" CELLSPACING="0" WIDTH="100%" STYLE="font: 11pt/115% Calibri, Helvetica, Sans-Serif; margin-top: 6pt; margin-bottom: 6pt; color: #0F2B89"><TR STYLE="vertical-align: top">
<TD STYLE="width: 18pt"></TD><TD STYLE="width: 19.5pt"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Gotham Rounded Book; font-size: 9pt"><B>C.</B></FONT></TD><TD STYLE="text-align: justify"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Gotham Rounded Book; font-size: 9pt"><B>Operating volumes </B></FONT></TD></TR></TABLE>

<P STYLE="font: 9pt Gotham Rounded Book; margin: 6pt 0; text-align: justify">In 2Q25, Central Puerto&rsquo;s power generation was 4,372
GWh, which implied a 24% decrease from 1Q25 of 5,731 GWh and a 12% decrease from 2Q24 of 4,985 GWh. Lower volumes in the quarter were
mainly explained by Mitsubishi&rsquo;s Central Costanera combined cycle and ST06 (TV06) from Central Puerto complex scheduled maintenance
and consequent downtime. Steam production increased 23% during 1Q25, reaching 930 thousand tons (ktn) compared to 756 ktn in the previous
quarter.</P>

<P STYLE="font: 9pt/115% Gotham Rounded Book; margin: 6pt 0; text-align: justify">The table below depicts operating figures for the second
quarter of 2025 (2Q25), compared to the previous quarter (Q125) and the same quarter of the previous year (2Q24):</P>

effect due to inflation exceeding currency depreciation during the period. As Central Puerto reports in Argentine pesos and converts figures
to US dollars at the end-of-period exchange rate, this mismatch may affect comparability.</P>

<P STYLE="font: 9pt Gotham Rounded Book; margin: 6pt 0; text-align: justify; color: #2D2D7E"><B>D.1. Revenues</B></P>

<P STYLE="font: 9pt/115% Gotham Rounded Book; margin: 6pt 0; text-align: justify">Revenues in 2Q25 totalized US$ 179.6 MM, which represented
an 8% decrease compared to 1Q25 (US$ 196.2 MM) and an increase of 7% compared to 2Q24 (US$ 168.3 MM). Revenues from energy sales represented
89.6% out of total revenues. Energy sales at 2Q25 were US$ 160.9 MM, a 10% decrease compared to 1Q25 (US$ 179.3 MM), and a 9% increase
compared to 2Q24 (US$ 147.2 MM). The q/q variation was <FONT STYLE="color: windowtext">primarily due to seasonal capacity charges (US$
-19.1 MM q/q) and lower volumes sold mainly from the maintenance work at the Mitsubishi combined cycle of Central Costanera and ST06 (TV06)
from Central Puerto complex (US$ -12.4 MM q/q), partly compensated by additional revenues from self-procured fuels for Luj&aacute;n de
Cuyo and other fuels (natural gas and liquids) as established by Resol. SE N&deg; 21/25 (+US$ 13.8 MM q/q). </FONT></P>

<P STYLE="font: 9pt Gotham Rounded Book; margin: 6pt 0; text-align: justify; color: #0F2B89"><B>E. Capital expenditures (capex)</B></P>

<P STYLE="font: 9pt Gotham Rounded Book; margin: 6pt 0; text-align: justify"><B>&nbsp;</B></P>

<P STYLE="font: 9pt Gotham Rounded Book; margin: 6pt 0; text-align: justify">Total capex in 1H 2025 was US$ 102.4 MM, mainly consisted
of the Brigadier L&oacute;pez combined cycle conversion, the San Carlos solar project, the additional subscription of shares from AbraSilver
and maintenance capex (mainly for the one-time major maintenance work in the Mitsubishi combined cycle from Central Costanera).</P>

<P STYLE="font: 9pt Gotham Rounded Book; margin: 6pt 0; text-align: justify"><B>&nbsp;</B></P>

<P STYLE="font: 9pt/115% Gotham Rounded Book; margin: 6pt 0; text-align: justify">Financial statements as of <B>June 30<SUP>th</SUP>,
2025, </B>include the effects of the inflation adjustment, applying IAS 29. Accordingly, the financial statements have been stated in
terms of the measuring unit current at the end of the reporting period, including the corresponding financial figures for previous periods
reported for comparative purposes. Comparative analysis refers to the same period of the previous year, measured in the current unit at
the end of the period, unless otherwise stated. Consequently, the information included in the Financial Statements for the second quarter
ended on <B>June 30<SUP>th</SUP>, 2025, </B>is not comparable to the Financial Statements previously published by the company. However,
we presented some figures converted from Argentine Pesos to U.S. dollars for comparison purposes only. The exchange rate used to convert
Argentine Pesos to U.S. dollars was the reference exchange rate (Communication &ldquo;A&rdquo; 3500) reported by the Central Bank for
U.S. dollars for the end of each period. The information presented in U.S. dollars is for the convenience of the reader only and may defer
if such conversion for each period is performed at the exchange rate applicable at the end of the latest period. You should not consider
these translations to be representations that the Argentine Peso amounts actually represent these U.S. dollars amounts or could be converted
into U.S. dollars at the rate indicated.</P>

<P STYLE="font: 9pt/115% Gotham Rounded Book; margin: 6pt 0; text-align: justify">Definitions and terms used herein are provided in the
Glossary at the end of this document. This release does not contain all the Company&rsquo;s financial information. As a result, investors
should read this release in conjunction with Central Puerto&rsquo;s Audited Consolidated Financial Statements for the fiscal period ended
on <B>December 31st, 2024, </B>and the notes thereto, which will be available on the Company&rsquo;s website.</P>

<P STYLE="font: 9pt Gotham Rounded Book; margin: 6pt 0; text-align: justify"><U>Rounding amounts and percentages</U>: Certain amounts
and percentages included in this release have been rounded <B>for</B> ease of presentation. Percentage figures included in this release
have not in all cases been calculated on the basis of such rounded figures, but on the basis of such amounts prior to rounding. For this
reason, certain percentage amounts in this release may vary from those obtained by performing the same calculations using the figures
in the financial statements. In addition, certain other amounts that appear in this release may not sum due to rounding.</P>

<P STYLE="font: 9pt Gotham Rounded Book; margin: 6pt 0; text-align: justify">This release contains certain metrics, including information
per share,&nbsp;operating information, and others, which do not have standardized meanings or standard methods of calculation and therefore
such measures may not be comparable to similar measures used by other companies. Such metrics have been included herein to provide readers
with additional measures to evaluate the Company&rsquo;s performance; however, such measures are not reliable indicators of the future
performance of the Company and future performance may not compare to the performance in previous periods.</P>

<P STYLE="font: 9pt Gotham Rounded Book; margin: 6pt 0; text-align: justify"><B>EBITDA and Adjusted EBITDA </B></P>

<P STYLE="font: 9pt Gotham Rounded Book; margin: 6pt 0; text-align: justify">In this release, <B>EBITDA</B>, a non-IFRS financial measure,
is defined as net income for the period, <I>plus</I> finance expenses, <I>minus</I> finance income, <I>minus </I>share of the profit (loss)
of associates, <I>plus (minus) </I>losses (gains) on net monetary position, <I>plus</I> income tax expense, <I>plus</I> depreciation and
amortization, <I>minus</I> net results of discontinued operations.</P>

<P STYLE="font: 9pt Gotham Rounded Book; margin: 6pt 0; text-align: justify"><B>Adjusted EBITDA</B> refers to EBITDA <I>excluding </I>impairment
on property, plant &amp; equipment, foreign exchange differences and interests related to FONI trade receivables and variations in fair
value of biological asset.</P>

<P STYLE="font: 9pt Gotham Rounded Book; margin: 6pt 0; text-align: justify">Adjusted EBITDA is believed to provide useful supplemental
information to investors about the Company and its results. Adjusted EBITDA is among the measures used by the Company&rsquo;s management
team to evaluate the financial and operating performance and make day-to-day financial and operating decisions. In addition, Adjusted
EBITDA is frequently used by securities analysts, investors, and other parties to evaluate companies in the industry. Adjusted EBITDA
is believed to be helpful to investors because it provides additional information about trends in the core operating performance prior
to considering the impact of capital structure, depreciation, amortization, and taxation on the results.</P>

<P STYLE="font: 9pt Gotham Rounded Book; margin: 6pt 0; text-align: justify">Adjusted EBITDA should not be considered in isolation or
as a substitute for other measures of financial performance reported in accordance with IFRS. Adjusted EBITDA has limitations as an analytical
tool, including:</P>

<TABLE CELLPADDING="0" CELLSPACING="0" WIDTH="100%" STYLE="font: 11pt/115% Calibri, Helvetica, Sans-Serif; margin-top: 6pt; margin-bottom: 6pt"><TR STYLE="vertical-align: top">
<TD STYLE="width: 0.25in"></TD><TD STYLE="width: 0.5in"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Symbol; font-size: 9pt">&#183;</FONT></TD><TD STYLE="text-align: justify"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Gotham Rounded Book; font-size: 9pt">Adjusted EBITDA does not reflect changes
in, including cash requirements for, working capital needs or contractual commitments. </FONT></TD></TR></TABLE>

<TABLE CELLPADDING="0" CELLSPACING="0" WIDTH="100%" STYLE="font: 11pt/115% Calibri, Helvetica, Sans-Serif; margin-top: 6pt; margin-bottom: 6pt"><TR STYLE="vertical-align: top">
<TD STYLE="width: 0.25in"></TD><TD STYLE="width: 0.5in"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Symbol; font-size: 9pt">&#183;</FONT></TD><TD STYLE="text-align: justify"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Gotham Rounded Book; font-size: 9pt">Adjusted EBITDA does not reflect the finance
expenses, or the cash requirements to service interest or principal payments on indebtedness, or interest income or other finance income.</FONT></TD></TR></TABLE>

<TABLE CELLPADDING="0" CELLSPACING="0" WIDTH="100%" STYLE="font: 11pt/115% Calibri, Helvetica, Sans-Serif; margin-top: 6pt; margin-bottom: 6pt"><TR STYLE="vertical-align: top">
<TD STYLE="width: 0.25in"></TD><TD STYLE="width: 0.5in"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Symbol; font-size: 9pt">&#183;</FONT></TD><TD STYLE="text-align: justify"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Gotham Rounded Book; font-size: 9pt">Adjusted EBITDA does not reflect income
tax expense or the cash requirements to pay income taxes.</FONT></TD></TR></TABLE>

<TABLE CELLPADDING="0" CELLSPACING="0" WIDTH="100%" STYLE="font: 11pt/115% Calibri, Helvetica, Sans-Serif; margin-top: 6pt; margin-bottom: 6pt"><TR STYLE="vertical-align: top">
<TD STYLE="width: 0.25in"></TD><TD STYLE="width: 0.5in"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Symbol; font-size: 9pt">&#183;</FONT></TD><TD STYLE="text-align: justify"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Gotham Rounded Book; font-size: 9pt">Although depreciation and amortization
are non-cash charges, the assets being depreciated or amortized often will need to be replaced in the future, and Adjusted EBITDA does
not reflect any cash requirements for these replacements. </FONT></TD></TR></TABLE>

<TABLE CELLPADDING="0" CELLSPACING="0" WIDTH="100%" STYLE="font: 11pt/115% Calibri, Helvetica, Sans-Serif; margin-top: 6pt; margin-bottom: 6pt"><TR STYLE="vertical-align: top">
<TD STYLE="width: 0.25in"></TD><TD STYLE="width: 0.5in"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Symbol; font-size: 9pt">&#183;</FONT></TD><TD STYLE="text-align: justify"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Gotham Rounded Book; font-size: 9pt">Although a certain share of the profit
of associates is a non-cash charge, Adjusted EBITDA does not consider the potential collection of dividends; and </FONT></TD></TR></TABLE>

<TABLE CELLPADDING="0" CELLSPACING="0" WIDTH="100%" STYLE="font: 11pt/115% Calibri, Helvetica, Sans-Serif; margin-top: 6pt; margin-bottom: 6pt"><TR STYLE="vertical-align: top">
<TD STYLE="width: 0.25in"></TD><TD STYLE="width: 0.5in"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Symbol; font-size: 9pt">&#183;</FONT></TD><TD STYLE="text-align: justify"><FONT STYLE="font-family: Gotham Rounded Book; font-size: 9pt">Other companies may calculate Adjusted
EBITDA differently, limiting its usefulness as a comparative measure. </FONT></TD></TR></TABLE>

<P STYLE="font: 9pt Gotham Rounded Book; margin: 6pt 0; text-align: justify">The Company compensates for the inherent limitations associated
with using Adjusted EBITDA through disclosure of these limitations, presentation of the Company&rsquo;s consolidated financial statements
in accordance with IFRS and reconciliation of Adjusted EBITDA to the most directly comparable IFRS measure, net income. For a reconciliation
of the net income to Adjusted EBITDA, see the tables included in this release.</P>

<P STYLE="font: 9pt Gotham Rounded Book; margin: 6pt 0; text-align: justify">All the information presented must be considered as consolidated
unless otherwise specified.</P>

<P STYLE="font: 9pt Gotham Rounded Book; margin: 6pt 0; text-align: justify">&nbsp;</P>
